Every historical requirement, accounted for.

139 features and 359 acceptance rows mapped to industry-wide delivery. Original wording, hashes and relationships remain intact.

Current delivery intent

All construction trades can use count, length, area and evidenced volume. Proven specialist packs add capabilities; fencing remains optional. Quantities, purchasing and exact AUD prices have separate reviewable boundaries. Local work requires no sign-in or database.

54 compatibility-fencing
27 superseded-scope
103 pending-analysis

Bounded next-slice proposals

Proposals allocate missing work to existing tasks; they do not start or complete those tasks.

IW002-P01 → IW-022

Multi-source transaction and legacy coexistence

Old job/run fields and single-document assumptions cannot be the construction persistence authority.

Exit proof: Create electrical and flooring workpackages with identical sheet numbers from different documents; restart and migrate a legacy fixture losslessly; inject transaction failures and stale command races.

Depends on IW-004 · Historical anchors: DATA-001, DATA-005, DC-048, DC-062, DC-063

IW002-P02 → IW-010

Count and length vertical journeys

Historical manual tools centre fence runs/gates and lack a first-class evidence-linked electrical count workflow.

Exit proof: Electrical count needs no calibration; pipe length requires verified units; duplicate contributions, changed sources and rejected evidence block. Prove edit/reload/review at desktop/mobile.

Depends on IW-022, IW-005 · Historical anchors: TRACE-002, TRACE-009, FC-006, RP-002

IW002-P03 → IW-011

Flooring area and concrete volume journeys

FC-002 allows removing area and no historical row fully specifies generic volume/depth provenance.

Exit proof: Floor polygon with explicit deductions and concrete area×evidenced-depth/native-property volume; wrong-source/property, invalid units, zero/underflow/overflow and revision change fail closed. Review and reload with no fencing fields.

Depends on IW-022, IW-005 · Historical anchors: FC-002, TRACE-004, FC-022, FC-031

IW002-P04 → IW-012

Generic review binding and repair loop

Run/gate approval cannot certify a changed calibration, source revision, depth specification or workpackage.

Exit proof: Immutable approve/reject history with optimistic concurrency; each binding mutation makes only dependent results stale; navigate blocker→source→repair→reapprove with captured evidence.

Depends on IW-010, IW-011 · Historical anchors: RP-001, RP-002, RP-003, RP-009, RP-031

IW002-P05 → IW-013

Proven pack manifest and fencing isolation

Old ordering requires fencing before every other trade; disabled specialist packs cannot be advertised as working.

Exit proof: Versioned capabilities, representative electrical plus one other trade corpus, deterministic outputs, isolated failure and optional legacy fencing module. Manual universal takeoff works with all specialist packs disabled.

Depends on IW-012 · Historical anchors: FC-017, FC-018, FC-023, FC-024, FC-025, FC-026, FC-027, FC-033

IW002-P06 → IW-014

Auditable AUD estimate vertical journey

Frozen historical pricing consumes fencing BOM; universal measured/purchase/price separation requires integration.

Exit proof: Import sourced AUD catalog, explicit mapping and purchase conversion; exact minor-unit total with labour/ancillary inputs, stale overrides and export/reopen. No default price or hidden allowance.

Depends on IW-012 · Historical anchors: PR-006, PR-020, PR-023, PR-024, PR-045

IW002-P07 → IW-005

Format-specific source corpus

PDF geometry, DXF text/transforms, SVG curves and IFC units/property identity were unresolved in old sources.

Exit proof: Bounded corpus with crop/rotation and two same-number sheets; distinguish supported manual rendering from automatic extraction. Declare IFC/OCR subset before enablement.

Depends on IW-004 · Historical anchors: FC-011, FC-028, FC-030, FC-031, FC-032

IW002-P08 → IW-017

Source-built native schema packaging and continuity

CLI source compatibility does not prove schema bundling, native execution or no-network restart.

Exit proof: IW-PACKAGE-SCHEMA bundles exact frozen schema digest, then package source-built sidecar and prove clean no-Python install, generic local workflow, network-denied restart and archive roundtrip.

Depends on IW-016, IW-015 · Historical anchors: DC-008, DC-011, DC-016, DC-038, DC-073, DC-084
